 relative_position_id = SFC_power_to_the_king

 id = SFC_strengthen_the_papacy icon = GFX_sof_focus_unique_sfc_strengthen_the_papacy
 
 
 
 
 cost = 5
 search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY FOCUS_FILTER_BALANCE_OF_POWER }
 available = { is_subject = no has_capitulated = no } cancel_if_invalid = yes continue_if_invalid = no
 
 ai_will_do = { factor = 1
  modifier = { factor = 1.8 has_stability < 0.45 }
 }

completion_reward = { if = { limit = {  is_subject = no has_capitulated = no  NOT = { has_country_flag = sof_rc_stage_sfc_strengthen_the_papacy } }
set_country_flag = sof_rc_stage_sfc_strengthen_the_papacy
if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = sof_rc_component_sfc_seek_papal_support } }
 set_country_flag = sof_rc_component_sfc_seek_papal_support
 add_popularity = { ideology = authoritarian_democrat popularity = 0.05 } add_ideas = sof_van_cor_ita_papal_support if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = SFC_disband_the_blackshirts } } 
}

 set_country_flag = sof_rc_component_sfc_agents_of_the_church
 if = { limit = { has_dlc = "La Resistance" } if = { limit = { has_idea = sof_van_cor_ita_papal_support } swap_ideas = { remove_idea = sof_van_cor_ita_papal_support add_idea = sof_van_cor_ita_papal_support_agents } } else = { add_ideas = sof_van_cor_ita_papal_support_agents } } else = { if = { limit = { has_idea = sof_van_cor_ita_papal_support } swap_ideas = { remove_idea = sof_van_cor_ita_papal_support add_idea = sof_van_cor_ita_papal_support_agents_no_lar } } else = { add_ideas = sof_van_cor_ita_papal_support_agents_no_lar } } 
}
if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = sof_rc_component_sfc_strengthen_the_papacy } }
 set_country_flag = sof_rc_component_sfc_strengthen_the_papacy
 if = { limit = { has_dlc = "La Resistance" } if = { limit = { has_idea = sof_van_cor_ita_papal_support_agents } swap_ideas = { remove_idea = sof_van_cor_ita_papal_support_agents add_idea = sof_van_cor_ita_papal_support_strengthened } } else = { add_ideas = sof_van_cor_ita_papal_support_strengthened } } else = { if = { limit = { has_idea = sof_van_cor_ita_papal_support_agents_no_lar } swap_ideas = { remove_idea = sof_van_cor_ita_papal_support_agents_no_lar add_idea = sof_van_cor_ita_papal_support_strengthened_no_lar } } else = { add_ideas = sof_van_cor_ita_papal_support_strengthened_no_lar } }
